A dropped miss moves a player's rate both ways: it may be his rebound, so his rate reads too low, and for every other player on the floor it is a chance he did not get, so his rate reads too high. The lane was not refit (PLAN.md D94, D95); the site withholds the player rates those drops could move by 0.5 per 100 chances or more, either way."}]}]]}]]}]

162:T417,Paired bootstrap by player (scouting) or by game (possession), 2000 resamples, 95 percent percentile interval of the difference. - H1, training helps: the dev-best fine-tuned arm minus A0, per kind and size. Passes when the difference is at least +0.10 and its lower bound is above 0; fails when the upper bound is below +0.10; otherwise inconclusive. - H2, the checker as a teacher: A2 minus A1. Lower bound above 0: rejection sampling adds to distillation; upper bound below 0: it hurts; otherwise inconclusive. - H3, near the API model (scouting only): the dev-best arm with the repair loop minus Haiku as delivered. Lower bound at or above -0.05: non-inferior; upper bound below -0.05: inferior; otherwise inconclusive. At 81 players the interval half-width of a pass-rate difference is about 0.1 near rates of 0.8, so inconclusive is a likely reading and is stated as such. - H4, constrained decoding: A3 minus its arm, on the number_value failure rate and on the pass rate. Read as pass, fail or inconclusive on the pass rate by the H2 rule.131:["$","details",null,{"className":"sources","children":[["$","summary",null,{"children":"The bar to adopt it, fixed in advance"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"SZJi2a_prose small","children":["$","span",null,{"data-file":"methods.json","data-field":"lanes[lane=genai].experiments[id=genai-E2].threshold","data-shown":"text","children":"$162"}]}]]}]
